atra viśeṣa

spaṣṭaṃ bhavet tadvalanaṃ khagasya
bhṛtyaśakastatra tatra yutāṃnā dṛkṣepake sāmyagata vilomā। sandhigrahaḥ syāt khalu tarasame stra graha bhujā naiva bhavaṃ khagasya ।। 142 ॥ tathā trimavya guṇita hṛtā''dya śruteijyayā lagnabhavā'graka । tatra phuṭākhyā valanajyakā yat samā samākhyāpamavṛttayośca ॥ 243 ॥ tatkālajā syāt paramāntarajyā yathā jinajyā viṣuvaṣamāntaḥ । samākhyavṛttaṃ viṣuvakhyavṛttaṃ bhavṛttakaṃ taddhi bhavṛttameva ।। 144 ॥ tayaśca yā sya। paramāntarajya। janajyakā sā'tha bhujā'pamayā । vimbadva yo'tra bhujaḥ sa ca syāt spaṣṭapamathopasuvṛtta taṃ tu ।। 145॥

mayeyaṃ śeṣavāsanā । kathitā tadvidāṃ prītyai subodhoskṛṣṭajīvinām ॥ 1 ॥ iti śrīsakalagaṇakasārvabhamanṛsiṃcamajakamalakaravira- citā śeṣavāsanā sampūrṇaṃ ॥ (1) bhāskaroktaṃ kanabimbamityādi । madhyakanābimbaṃ vivyaṃ śamuce nīce savyaṃśaminya cartha haro'yaṃ 1। 30 । nīcathaṃ ga- ṇaścaya 1 । 20 । nahyak caraguṇakavatra guṇacarā madhyayājana kathayāmuccana cayojanakaśajñānārthamupayuktaṃ na vā sarvakheṭe dhvaivameva paro guṇaka cetyayuktaṃ taskarabimbam । kaśāgyakalā mityā bimbasya siddhatvāttajjasthirā''dyānayane kalanat tadpa vena gatyantarānupātānītvāhaṭikadisthisyādyanupapatteśca । (2) yenaiva bhakta karaṇatyādi । atra karaṇī yayaśnatasta yorāyatabhujakoṭyoryagatu yavyāsasya vṛtte tadasyotkramajyasa mbandhi kramajyaiva karaṇapadaṃ yato bonanavyāso'tra bhujako ṭighāta eva karaṇarūpastatpadaṃ do krasajyetyuktaṃ prāka। atha ka raṇe tu bhajakoṭighātaḥ sa tanmūlayorvargaghātaḥ samatvāgmūlaghā tavargaghātaḥ samatvānmūlaghātavarga eveti karaṇapadaṃ mūlaghāta iti siddham ।
